<script async="" src="https://account.bonnier.news/bip-sdk-1.0.js" type="text/javascript"></script>
<script>

            (function () {
                if (diConf.isForetagPage) {
                    return;
                }

                var numberOfChecks = 0;
                const html = document.querySelector('html');

                var checkAdBlockEnabled = function () {

                    var firstAdElement = document.querySelector('[data-line-item-ids]');
                    if (firstAdElement) {
                        clearInterval(detectionInterval);
                        localStorage.setItem('browserHasAdblocker', false);
                        return;
                    }

                    numberOfChecks++;
                    if (numberOfChecks > 15) {
                        clearInterval(detectionInterval);
                        localStorage.setItem('browserHasAdblocker', true);
                        if (html.classList.contains('logged-out')) {
                            runAdBlockPaywall();
                        }
                    }
                };

                var runAdBlockPaywall = function () {
                    var trackingData = { referrer: document.referrer, utmTag: '', backendPaywall: "true" };
                    if (window.location.href.indexOf('utm_source') > -1) {
                        trackingData.utmTag = window.location.href;
                    }

                    var articleId = '';
                    var element = document.querySelector("[data-articleid]");
                    if (element) {
                        articleId = element.getAttribute("data-articleid");
                    }

                    var paywalljs = document.createElement("script");
                    paywalljs.setAttribute("id", "serviceplusPaywall3");
                    paywalljs.setAttribute("src", window.diConf.servicePlusPaywallUrl);
                    paywalljs.setAttribute('data-articleid', articleId);
                    paywalljs.setAttribute("data-appId", window.diConf.servicePlusAppId);
                    paywalljs.setAttribute("data-resourceId", window.diConf.servicePlusResourceId);
                    paywalljs.setAttribute("data-forcehit", "true");
                    paywalljs.setAttribute("data-variant-type", "ADBLOCKER");
                    paywalljs.setAttribute("data-loginURL", window.diConf.servicePlusLoginUrl);
                    paywalljs.setAttribute("data-logoutURL", window.diConf.servicePlusLogoutUrl);
